And telling me I shouldn't touch certain cars in the shop. So... yeah. That's what's going on.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced anything unusual with your vision, like seeing things that aren't there, in addition to hearing the voice?\n\nPatient: No. Um... no, I haven't seen anything like that. It's just... just the voice.\n\nDoctor: Does the voice tell you to do specific things or just express opinions about your husband and work?\n\nPatient: Um... well, like I said... it tells me not to touch certain cars in the shop. So... yeah, I guess it tells me specific things. It just says... don't touch it. And... to not trust my husband. That's... that's really all it says.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have to do what the voice tells you to do?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I don't... it doesn't force me or anything. I just... I usually just leave those cars alone anyway when it says that. But... no. I don't have to.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling sad or depressed lately?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I don't... I don't think so. Just... normal, I guess.\n\nDoctor: How long have you been hearing the voice?\n\nPatient: Um... a while. I guess... maybe about ten months? Yeah... around ten months since the voice started, and... when I noticed the tracking stuff. It's been about that long.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe what the voice says about your husband is true?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I do. I know it sounds... far-fetched, I guess. But... I'm still convinced. Him and... the shop owner. They've been using the EV chargers and... the security cameras to track what I'm doing. They try to show me the footage and say there's nothing on it, but... I just know they are.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever find it hard to express your thoughts or feel like your thoughts are disjointed or don't make sense to others?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I don't... I don't think so. People seem to understand me fine.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your husband and the shop owner are tracking you outside of work as well?\n\nPatient: Um... I don't really know. I haven't... I guess I haven't noticed it anywhere else. Just... mostly just at the shop.\n\nDoctor: Do you think there's anything else going on that's not quite right, aside from what the voice tells you about your husband and the tracking?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. Just... just the stuff at the shop. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband and the shop owner tracking you is true, even when the voice is not telling you this?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. I mean... I know it's true. \n\nEven when... like, there were a few weeks a little while ago where things were a lot quieter. I was just... sleeping a lot more. I wasn't really doing much or making huge plans or anything... but I still heard the voice in the garage. And I still knew my husband and my boss were tracking me. So... yes.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period in your life where you felt extremely happy, energetic, or irritable and didn't need much sleep?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess so. Sometimes. \n\nLike... sometimes I feel really, really good. Like I could... I don't know, fix every car in the shop, all by myself. Just... going and going. But then... if my husband says something, like about the dishes, or if one of the other techs touches my tools... I just... I get really mad. Like they're trying to stop me or hold me back. So... yeah, sometimes.\n\nDoctor: When you feel really, really good and energetic, does that usually last for a few days or more at a time?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah. It lasts a while. \n\nLike... for a long time now. Maybe... almost a year? Eleven months, I think. Just... long stretches where I don't sleep much. Always moving, always making plans. It just... keeps going.\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling really good and energetic, do you ever do things that you later regret?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I mean, I don't think so. I just... I do what needs to be done. I don't really look back and regret it.\n\nDoctor: When you're feeling really, really good and energetic, do you need less sleep than you normally would?\n\nPatient: Yeah. I guess. \n\nLike... for weeks at a time, I'd maybe get two or three hours. I just... wake up before dawn. My head's already at work, already in the garage. I just feel like... sleeping more is just a waste of time. So I just get up.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
